540: function townSymbol(type){
541:   return {healer:'✚',care:'✚',lab:'⚗',station:'🚉',shop:'◆',arena:'★',museum:'◉',school:'✎',inn:'☾'}[type]||''
542: }
543: function roofColor(type,theme){
544:   if(type==='healer'||type==='care')return '#d95b62';
545:   if(type==='lab')return '#7b7fd8';
546:   if(type==='station')return '#536f8c';
547:   if(type==='shop')return '#d6a849';
548:   if(type==='arena')return '#d07549';
549:   if(type==='museum')return '#9b7a64';
550:   if(type==='school')return '#5d8bb8';
551:   if(type==='inn')return '#6e8a63';
552:   return theme.roof
553: }
